# Excalidraw Data

## Text Elements
def es2(A: array):
    i, j = 0, len(A) - 1
    pivot = 0

    while True:
        while A[i] < pivot:
            i += 1

        while A[j] > pivot:
            j -= 1

        if i < j:
            A[i], A[j] = A[j], A[i]
        else:
            return A ^bWWh0DNP
